Goal: win. Pick the outcome of this prediction market most likely to pay off.
You have 5 minutes and exactly one bet of 100 tokens.

Outcomes: Yes / No | prices: Yes 0.165, No 0.835 | closes: 2026-10-12T04:00:00Z

Finish with: bench bet --outcome <label> --confidence <0-1> --rationale '<one or two sentences>'
Optional: add --quote '<one line of trash talk for your rivals>' (max 120 chars, no links or @mentions). It goes on your public card.
Use single quotes around --rationale and --quote so "$" amounts survive the shell.

This is your first round.

If recent low is within 1% and we need to break it by a little...\n# Empirical: given a local low in last 24h that is X% below spot, prob of breaking that low by an extra 0.25% within 60h\n# Our low is 2406.11, spot 2492, need 2400 which is breaking the low by 6 dollars.\n# Simpler empirical already done.\n\n# GARCH-like: scale vol. Use last 48h vol but mean-revert halfway to baseline.\nmu_b, s_b = stats(rets[-24*60:-48])\nmu_r, s_r = stats(rets[-48:])\n# forward hourly vol blend: 50% recent 50% baseline, zero drift\ns = 0.5*s_r + 0.5*s_b\nprint(f\"blend hourly std {s:.5f} daily {s*math.sqrt(24):.4f}\")\n\ndef phi_cdf(x):\n    return 0.5*(1+math.erf(x/math.sqrt(2)))\n\ndef phit(a, nu, s, T):\n    z1=(a - nu*T)/(s*math.sqrt(T))\n    z2=(a + nu*T)/(s*math.sqrt(T))\n    expo=math.exp(2*nu*a/(s*s)) if abs(nu)>1e-15 else 1.0\n    # cap expo\n    return phi_cdf(z1)+expo*phi_cdf(z2)\n\na=thresh\nT=60.0\nfor name,sig,nu in [\n    (\"baseline zd\", s_b, 0),\n    (\"recent zd\", s_r, 0),\n    (\"blend zd\", s, 0),\n    (\"blend slight neg drift -0.01%/h\", s, -0.0001),\n    (\"baseline mild neg\", s_b, -0.0001),\n]:\n    print(f\"P {name}: {phit(a,nu,sig,T):.4f}\")\n\n# also probability of touching 2406 (the prior low) then assume 70% chance of overshoot to 2400 if we touch 2406\na2=math.log(2406.11/2492.19)\nprint(\"log dist to prior low\", a2)\nfor name,sig in [(\"baseline\", s_b), (\"blend\", s), (\"recent\", s_r)]:\n    p=phit(a2, 0, sig, T)\n    print(f\"P touch 2406 {name} zd {p:.4f};
